### 93 subject events

- **1915.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Klara踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Klara抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Klara举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Klara站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Grete Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Klara把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Klara正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000438:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Klara前面的大婶一直往后靠，Klara就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Klara的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill_

- **1919.05** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-scarlet` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  阁楼的窗子被Josef Bauer用布堵了一半，说是怕风。Klara躺在草垫上，听见楼下有人问“你家孩子怎么样了”，Josef Bauer压低声音回话。医生来了一趟，站在门口没进屋，诊费是一克朗二十赫勒。此后七天，Klara的碗和水壶都放在门外。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (付医生诊费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Klara空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Josef Bauer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Klara用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Klara把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Josef Bauer。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994; food; skill_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Anna Moser把Klara拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Klara把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Klara蹲下把两包并排，按Anna Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Klara推刨子。Klara推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Klara，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Rosa把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Klara留在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Klara在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。

- **1932.07** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Klara和Mitzi Lechner合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-mitzi-lechner; household; mood 0.06_

- **1932.09** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000128:child; reputation -0.02_

  _effects: cash -1 (借来的硬领); flag +work13_prepared_for_interview; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1933.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外是维也纳第十五区的一条窄街，卖烤栗子的炉子冒着白烟。Klara贴完纸退后两步看，发现“欠”字被浆糊洇开了一点。老成员没说话，只把眼镜摘下来擦了又擦。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1933.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Grete Kraus嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Grete Kraus家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Grete Kraus那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000128:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1933.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Mitzi Weiss拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000146:child_

  _effects: cash -5 (板车上的十二箱（其中5克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +work13_delivery_ruined_by_rain; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  Josef Berg问Anna Moser在哪儿住，Klara说就在后院那间偏房，住了六年，房租每月初一交。Josef Berg翻到登记页，发现门牌号少了一个字母，还是签了。Anna Moser出来时，把手里攥着的一小块肥皂塞给Klara，说刚从收容所领的。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +testified_for_neighbor_against_vagrancy_charge; role; relation friend with person-anna-moser; knowledge “the neighbor has rented the back room for six years”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  _effects: mood 0.05999999999999994; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

  _effects: role; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

  走到桥洞前，Franz Lechner说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Klara站在原地看着Franz Lechner拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; flag +uncertain_courtship_

Born 1912.01, died 1932.10 (age 20).
Education: secondary 2; occupation: none; roles: infant, child, child_worker, apprentice.

- **1930.04** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-concert-ticket` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Mitzi不再让Anna Bauer传话，托其带去一张星期天音乐会的站票，花了一克朗二十赫勒，位置在柱子后头。票上不写多余的字，只说若不方便，票留着也无妨。Anna Bauer把票揣进衬衫内袋，Mitzi则把找回来的铜板一枚枚放进铁盒，等一个回音。

  Mitzi熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

  Mitzi喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Anna Stein在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi没有当场求答复，而是绕到Anna Moser 2家门口，请这位常在院子里晒被子的邻居替他去问一声：能不能先请对方的姑妈来家里坐一趟，把见面的规矩谈清楚。Anna Moser 2答应了，说周三下午有空，但提醒他屋里得收拾干净，还得备一壶咖啡。Mitzi算着那壶咖啡要花掉八个赫勒。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; cash -0.08 (预备待客的咖啡和糖); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  Elisabeth Winter 2在门口替Mitzi捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  _effects: cash 12 (预支工资，先拿到现金); reputation 0.05_

- **1932.10** `vienna-life-work-injury/fatal-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, death
  压板落下时Mitzi没能把手抽出来。工友把人抬到门口，等救护车到时已经没有了呼吸；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne当天下午派人送来三十克朗抚恤金，车间里的机器停了一个钟头，又响起来，像什么都没有发生过。
  _effects: death (crushing injury at the printing press); cash 30 (employer death benefit)_

  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Josef用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Josef Bauer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Josef的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。

  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Josef站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Maria Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Josef把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Josef正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000998:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1923.11** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Otto Weiss擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Josef把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Otto Weiss看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000148:child_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:craft:pursuing_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/trial-promised` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Josef在印刷厂门口等到工头出来，把姓名、住址和会写的字报了一遍。Katharina Kral让他下周一早来试半天，先不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; flag +apprenticeship_trial_promised; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/trial-granted` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  “认得字吗？”Rosa Lechner问。Josef把地址翻到背面，念出门牌号。Rosa Lechner这才让Josef去码纸，按斤数分成三堆。Josef蹲到腿麻，手指被纸边割破，Rosa Lechner看完斤两，数出一克朗二十赫勒，说午饭在灶间吃，试工三天再谈工钱。
  _effects: cash 1.2 (试工半日按小时折算的铜币); food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1926.01** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Maria Lechner在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Josef下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josef跟着Otto Kral走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/kept-at-home` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship
  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Josef留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework)_

- **1927.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Moser 2把一条绷带绕在Josef的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Josef在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Anton Moser 2发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”_

  Josef交不起住宿费，校长让Josef每天清早去厨房帮工抵账。Josef四点起来削土豆，手上冻出裂口，上课时打瞌睡被Anton Stein点名。期末，Josef的操行栏写着“勤勉”，成绩却掉到中游。第二学期Josef的操行还是“勤勉”，成绩单寄回家，家里只看了学费那栏。

  Josef把用过的课本捆成一摞，提到旧书摊。Emil Kral一本本翻，把缺页的挑出来，最后给了五克朗。Josef接过钱，看见自己写在页边的笔记被Emil Kral随手撕掉，想说什么，还是没说，只把撕下的纸片捡了回来。纸片被油墨粘住，展开了。
  _effects: cash 5 (卖掉的课本); mood -0.03_

  Josef和Ferdinand Lechner在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Ferdinand Lechner在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood 0.04000000000000001; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中3.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood 0.05_

  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Otto Kral把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  Josef把听来的话添了一句，讲给Otto Kral听。三天后，Ferdinand Lechner在集市上被人拦住问，回家把篮子摔在桌上。Josef说只是随口一提，Ferdinand Lechner说随口的话才最像真的。

  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

  Josef把空煤筐拎到隔壁门口，说只借一筐，等手头松了就还。Emil Winter看了看筐底的锈，进屋铲了半筐，又从灶边拿了两块引火的柴。Josef道谢时，Emil Winter说这条走廊里借东西要记着，不是记在纸上。
  _effects: food; condition; flag +borrowed_coal_from_neighbour; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1941.02** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.returned-sugar-box/sugar-box-go-in-person` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Josef亲自去Otto Hofer家，把话说清：糖盒既退回来，以后不再托人登门。Otto Hofer的母亲站在门里，说这都是为自家孩子打算，语气不重也不轻。Josef把帽子戴正，没有纠缠，下楼时一步一步数着台阶；门房和邻居都看在眼里，这一回至少把话说在了明处。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; reputation 0.03_

- **1941.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef上楼敲了Otto Lechner的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Otto Lechner把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Josef能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.seek-friend::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::seek-friend-war/raid-friend` tags: task, social, friendship, loneliness, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Hofer在防空洞口分给Josef半支烟，两人背靠潮湿的砖墙，听远处炮声。Josef说自己怕的不是炮，是回家后没人说话；Otto Hofer把烟头按灭，说那就每天这个时辰在这儿等。
  _effects: condition; flag +air_raid_friend; knowledge “the cellar entrance is quiet after the all-clear”_

- **1942.02** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1942.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  配给秤的托盘底下粘着一块铅，Josef把面包放上去时，指针总比别处少半格。Emil Huber说秤是官家的，Josef让他把铅块抠下来；后面排队的人都不说话，只有Lena把自己的面包掰了一半递过来。
  _effects: food; flag +exposed_ration_scale_weight; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1942.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1942.07** `r1-hc.wound-infection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::infection-war-dirty-dressing/infection-war-clean-dressing` tags: health, infection, wound, hospital, delay, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef托人从黑市买来一小包新纱布，自己换药。伤口慢慢收口，留下一道发亮的疤；同屋的伤员还在用旧绷带，夜里一直哼哼，他把剩下的纱布藏在枕头下，没敢分。
  _effects: cash -10 (红线爬过手腕); condition; flag +bought_black_market_dressings; role; knowledge “clean dressings could be bought outside the hospital”_

- **1942.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Hermann Lechner谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Hermann Lechner说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Rosa Stein听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1942.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef先给Grete Bauer盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Grete Bauer吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000438:child; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  Josef和Otto Hofer在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Otto Hofer在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; mood 0.03999999999999998; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1943.05** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty

  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; flag +rent_increase_accepted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; condition_

  地窖的墙渗水，Gustav Winter把脚踏机架在两只啤酒箱上，印一页要踩十七下。Josef负责把油墨味盖住，在楼梯口煮一锅酸菜。印到半夜，Alois Hofer来取纸，说电车查得紧，只能夹在鞋垫底下带出去。
  _effects: cash -2 (地窖里的脚踏印刷机); flag +printed_underground_sheet; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001680:child; knowledge “the press is hidden behind the beer crates in the cellar”_

- **1949.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.no-baptism` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  教区的办事员翻了很久簿子才开出证明，纸上的墨还没干透。Josef一路攥着往南站赶，月台上卸货的人已经排成两行，他站在队尾，听人说上午就招满了。
  _effects: cash -1 (往返教区的车钱); mood -0.06999999999999995_

- **1949.02** `r13-turn.refugee-arrival::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-displaced-persons/turned-away` tags: migration, refugee, papers, housing, displacement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Berg说今天的号发完了，让Josef明天再来。Josef问能不能在走廊里等到天亮，Katharina Berg说这里不许过夜。Josef把湿行李拖到街边，坐在门口的石阶上，看见里面的人一队队领到热汤。Katharina Berg关窗时，把百叶帘拉了下来。
  _effects: cash -3 (登记处门外的湿行李); condition; flag +refused_refugee_registration; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000373:child; knowledge “the registration office stops issuing numbers before noon”_

- **1949.05** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001309:child; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06_

- **1949.10** `r13-rel.betrayal-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/confronted-in-the-tavern` tags: relationship, friend, betrayal, work, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在酒馆堵住Josef Kraus，把抄件拍在桌上。Josef Kraus先说不是自己，后来又说家里等钱用。Josef把杯子里的酒泼在Josef Kraus脸上，老板过来赶人。两个人从此在街上遇见都绕开走。
  _effects: mood -0.12; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000333:child_

- **1949.10** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008675_

- **1950.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Josef和Alois Hofer在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Alois Hofer把煤钱推过来，Josef接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-benefit-office/sent-to-relief-work` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Huber 3说补助金只发给有家属的人，Josef单身，只能去修路队领工钱。第二天Josef扛着铁锹站在城郊，和几十个男人一起把碎砖填进车辙。工钱按日结，比补助多两克朗，但一天下来，手掌磨出了三个水泡。
  _effects: cash 2 (补助金窗口的第二张表); condition; flag +work13_sent_to_relief_work_

- **1950.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Franz Lechner用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; flag +parish_registered_

- **1950.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Friedrich Bauer 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1950.11** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/understudy-contract` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Rosa 2演主角，Josef拿到的是替角合同：国王的戏服先按主角的尺寸做，Josef只能在排练时穿。导演说这不是坏事，至少不用负责谢幕。首演夜，Josef坐在后台，听见前台每一次掌声都像在敲自己的门。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:88; cash 1 (替角的排练补贴); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +understudy_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  新排的戏没有坐满。Josef在第二幕听见后排有人笑，不是笑台词，是笑那顶歪掉的王冠。散场后，经理说下一轮要换一个更年轻的国王。
  _effects: state_parameter stage-sovereign.publicBelief 5 -> 0; state_parameter stage-sovereign.scandal 0 -> 6; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1951.01** `kernel-civic.petition-drive::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-petition-refused` tags: politics, petition, reform, neighbor, organization, vienna, urban, postwar
  他把签名纸送到市政厅，Berta Huber 3说格式不对，要按街道分开。他回去重抄，发现有几个名字已经认不出；再去时，窗口换了一个人，说前一班没有交接。他坐在市政厅台阶上重抄，风把纸吹到花坛里。
  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸的纸笔钱); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分页，窗口换班时前一班可能没有交接”_

- **1951.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Josef把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000333:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Berg把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Josef在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Berta Winter 3分给Josef半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.12; flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

- **1952.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  汤锅冒着热气，Josef盛出两勺倒进Anna Lechner带来的罐子里。Anna Lechner说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001037:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.06** `r2-uni.readers-letters::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/returned-the-letter` tags: media, newspaper, letters, verification, reputation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef没有登那封信，而是骑车去了Franz Hofer家。两个人坐在厨房里，Franz Hofer承认自己是气不过儿子落选。Josef建议Franz Hofer把信改成给教育局的询问，Franz Hofer照做了。半年后，邮局多了一个公开招考的名额。
  _effects: flag +returned_readers_letter; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001337:child_

- **1953.01** `r13-fam.keep-family-secret::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/secret-kept` tags: task, family, secret, reputation, sibling, fear, vienna, urban
  Emil Stein 3在楼道里拦住Josef，问家里那张盖红章的纸是不是出了事。Josef说只是旧房租收据，回家却把纸从柜底翻出来，和Anton Bauer一起塞进米缸夹层。两人把米重新铺平，Anton Bauer的手一直在抖；Josef说这事到此为止，谁也别再提。
  _effects: condition; flag +hid_family_paper_in_rice_bin; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001309:child; knowledge “the paper is hidden between the rice bin’s layers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

- **1953.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.upstairs` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

- **1954.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Mitzi Berg从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.05 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002071:child_

- **1954.04** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-and-mother-weak` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury
  孩子出生后取名Josef Bauer，Rosa 2一直出血。Josef跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Rosa 2落下产后虚弱的毛病，Josef抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.2 (postpartum bleeding); cash -18 (doctor and medicine); condition_

- **1954.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Elisabeth Hofer把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Elisabeth Hofer在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi Berg嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Mitzi Berg家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Mitzi Berg那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002071:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  Josef没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010415_

  Franz Hofer把样布铺在装土豆的木箱上，先问Josef一个月能卖几匹。Josef报了实数，没有夸大，Franz Hofer反而点头，说头一批货可以三十天后再结。走的时候，Josef把样布角剪下一小块，回家对着灯比了半宿。
  _effects: flag +work13_supplier_gave_credit; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001337:child; knowledge “the supplier extends thirty days of credit to small shops”_

  Josef把通知念给Lena听，Lena摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Helene Bauer收下了锅，却没有让Josef进门，说东西留下，人先回去。Josef在门口站了一会儿，把锅盖掀开又盖上；第二天，空锅被放在门槛上，里面压着一张写着“收到了”的纸条。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000707:child; role; flag +cold_peace; knowledge “对方只收下东西，没有让进门，也没有提那笔钱。”_

  Josef没有转会，仍旧把硬币交给Anna Stein 2。三个月后，Anna Stein 2说公司被互助会并了，旧卡只能按半价转。Josef把卡翻来覆去看，打孔的地方已经连成一条线。Rosa 2说早该换，Josef说换不换都一样。
  _effects: cash -4 (每周二十赫勒的死亡); flag +kept_lapsed_industrial_policy; knowledge “the old policy transferred at half value after the merger”_

- **1958.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-gives-up` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在柜台前站了很久，最后把处方留在那里，说等有货再来。回家路上经过医院，看见药房窗口也挂着“无货”；第二天，处方被雨水泡烂在口袋里。Josef把湿透的处方摊在炉边烤，字还是糊了。
  _effects: cash -1 (tram fare and a paper envelope for the prescription); condition; role; knowledge “医院药房也会挂“无货”；处方留在柜台，第二天下雨就泡烂了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-177_

- **1959.01** `r14-hist.reconstruction-housing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/repaired-the-cellar-first` tags: reconstruction_housing, housing, postwar, repair, vienna, urban, reform, scarcity
  重建贷款只够修屋顶，Josef决定先修地窖，因为邻居家的煤和土豆都堆在那里。Anna Stein 2教Josef怎么把旧砖上的砂浆敲掉，再用石灰重新砌。地下室干了三天，墙上还是渗水；Josef在角落里放了一只铁盆，夜里听见水滴进去，像有人在数拍子。
  _effects: cash -18 (废墟上的三层砖); condition; flag +cellar_repaired; skill_

- **1959.04** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::liberation-anniversary/cleared-of-collaboration` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, postwar
  解放一周年，广场上立了一块临时木牌，写着“感谢”。Josef发现自己的名字被刻在“合作者”那一栏，因为战时在占领军厨房干过活。Emil 2替Josef作证，说Josef往汤里多兑了水，救过不少人；Grete 2听完，让人把木牌上的字刨掉。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.07999999999999999; flag +cleared_of_collaboration_charge; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002806:child; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001922:child; knowledge “my name was carved under collaborators”_

- **1959.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Maria Kraus在午休时把本子摊在Josef的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Maria Kraus当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000227:child; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1959.12** `r3-war.veterans-hall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/joined-veterans-association` tags: war, veterans, association, organization, postwar, vienna, urban, reform
  Grete Bauer把名册摊在酒馆后屋，让Josef在“伤兵”和“未伤”两栏里选。Josef说自己在战壕里冻掉两个脚趾，Grete Bauer说那不算伤；Franz Berg在旁边敲了敲杯子，说“算不算，先喝一杯”。
  _effects: flag +joined_veterans_association; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000438:child; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-170; organization_membership; knowledge “the association separates wounded from unwounded”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-170_

- **1960.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1960.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Josef的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Josef被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”_

- **1960.02** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Ferdinand Moser 2提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160_

- **1961.01** `r3-mig.exile-return::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/return-visa-granted` tags: migration, exile, return, politics, papers, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Weiss把审查表翻到第三页，问Josef在国外参加过什么组织。Josef说只去过一次救济会。Hermann Weiss在“无政治活动”一栏打了勾，给了一张回国许可，说“先去警察局报到”。Anna 2在门口等着，手里提着一瓶酒。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +return_visa_granted; knowledge “returning exiles must report to the police”_

- **1961.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::foster-postwar/orphanage-full` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  孤儿院的院长说孩子太多，床不够，让Josef再等一个月。Helene Weiss 3把Luise 2领回临时收容所，Josef每周去看一次。孩子渐渐肯说话了，却总是在Josef起身时抓住衣角，问下一次什么时候来。
  _effects: cash -2 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); condition; flag +waited_for_foster_placement; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:010837:child_

- **1961.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Grete Bauer在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000438:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1961.03** `r3-mig.family-reunification::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sibling-signed-affidavit` tags: migration, family, sponsorship, papers, debt, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa 2把担保书翻到最后一页，指着连带责任那一行让Helene Bauer读。Helene Bauer读完，还是签了名，说“总不能让Josef一个人在那边”。Rosa 2收下纸，给了一张入境许可的号码牌。Helene Bauer出门时把号码牌塞进Josef手里，手心全是汗。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +sibling_signed_affidavit; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000707:child; knowledge “the sponsor accepts joint liability for housing and support”_

- **1961.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Maria Huber的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Maria Huber提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1962.01** `r13-civ.dentist::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/tooth-pulled` tags: health, dentist, pain, cash, craft, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Moser 2让Josef咬住一块木楔，把钳子伸进嘴里，脚蹬着椅背使劲。牙根断在牙龈里，Ferdinand Moser 2又换了一把小凿子。出门时Josef的半个脸已经肿起来，雪落在脸上，反而觉得凉得舒服。
  _effects: cash -2 (拔牙钳与门外的雪); condition; flag +tooth_extracted; knowledge “the tooth root broke and had to be chiselled out”_

- **1962.10** `vienna-postwar-tv-cafe/watch-at-the-cafe` tags: adulthood, postwar, social, consumer
  Katharina Lechner来敲门，说街角咖啡馆新装了一台电视，今晚有足球转播。Josef跟着挤进烟雾缭绕的后屋，站着看完下半场；回家时还在学解说员的口气。
  _effects: cash -2 (coffee and entry); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004796:child; skill_

- **1963.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Elisabeth Moser 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Elisabeth Moser 2敲开Josef的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-157; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-157_

- **1963.01** `r13-fam.ask-neighbour-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/borrowed-coal` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef把空煤筐拎到隔壁门口，说只借一筐，等手头松了就还。Luise 2看了看筐底的锈，进屋铲了半筐，又从灶边拿了两块引火的柴。Josef道谢时，Luise 2说这条走廊里借东西要记着，不是记在纸上。
  _effects: food_

- **1963.02** `r2-leisure.kitchen-shortweight::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/told-the-housekeeper` tags: domestic_service, kitchen, theft, accounting, loyalty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在楼梯口追上Gustav Winter 2，说称可能不准。Gustav Winter 2没回头，只说明天换一杆秤。第二天Josef被调去擦银器，Berta Weiss 2把一锅热水泼在门口，谁也没提黄油。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; flag +reported_kitchen_shortweight; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; knowledge “the housekeeper will replace the butter scale without naming”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1963.04** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Friedrich Winter却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Josef把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-189; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-189_

- **1963.11** `vienna-postwar-supermarket/stall-closes` tags: adulthood, postwar, consumer, food
  常去买菜的摊子关了，摊主说租金涨得比他卖土豆赚得快。Josef改去新开的自助商店，货更整齐，却没人再替他挑最熟的那颗西红柿。
  _effects: food; cash -4 (packaged vegetables); mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +market_stall_closed_

- **1964.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1964.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/pharmacy-aspirin-substitute` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  药房说没有货，Josef把处方叠好，去了下一家。天黑时手里只有一包阿司匹林和一瓶咳嗽糖浆；Otto Lechner说总比空手强，把糖浆让给了孩子。回家后他把阿司匹林分成两半，糖浆瓶口用纸塞着，漏了一点。
  _effects: cash -8 (aspirin and cough syrup after a day of searching); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1964.05** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Emil 2手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Emil 2把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002806:child_

- **1964.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.short-jacket` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Josef把外套的肩线拆开，往里收了四指，袖口剪掉一截，改出一件到胯骨的短上衣。剩下的布头拼成两只手套，针脚粗，戴着能挡风。她在镜子前转了一圈，肩还是有点空，但总算能穿出门。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1964.11** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002959:child; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.05999999999999994_

- **1965.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-returned` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban
  信过了三个星期被退回来，信封上盖着“查无此人”，边角已经磨毛。Berta Weiss 2不认得那四个字，让Josef念；Josef念成“还在路上”，把信折好放进抽屉。Berta Weiss 2点点头，第二天又问有没有回信。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); mood -0.12; flag +letter_returned_undeliverable; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1965.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi 2嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Mitzi 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Mitzi 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007859:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1966.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, postwar
  Elisabeth Berg把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Josef在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Franz Huber 4分给Josef半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.12; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-206_

- **1966.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Josef把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; skill_

- **1966.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Berta Weiss 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1966.09** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.listen-coins` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1967.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/soup-kitchen-opened` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef把工会活动室的长桌拼起来，用两口大锅煮扁豆汤。Maria Kraus赊了半袋豆子和一筐洋葱，条件是罢工结束后先还他。第一天来了三十七个人，Josef给每个碗里多舀半勺；汤不够时，就往锅里再添一壶水。
  _effects: cash -1 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (罢工第七天的汤锅); flag +work13_strike_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000227:child; organization_funds_

  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Josef靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-186; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-186_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-179; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-179_

  Rosa Lechner把一只小篮子挂在Rosa脖子上，说捡满一篮就回家。葡萄行里的刺划破手指，Rosa用嘴吸了吸，继续捡。Katharina Kral过秤时多给了半个铜板，说这孩子手快。
  _effects: cash 1 (八岁的手和镰刀); flag +child_harvest_labor; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/folds-notices` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一捆教区通告交给Rosa Lechner带回家。Rosa放学后在厨房桌上折页到夜里，手指沾满油墨，两周后交回六千张，拿到三克朗二十赫勒；钱交给Rosa Lechner时，Rosa的指尖已经洗不干净，指甲缝里留着一圈黑；家里从此多了一个能挣几个铜板的孩子。
  _effects: cash 3.2 (folding parish notices); condition; role_

- **1921.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Rosa在第三圈超过Anna Moser，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Rosa口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Anna Moser从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-bought-secondhand` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  Rosa Lechner把八个赫勒排成三摞，最后还差两枚，向摊主说了句好话，才把石板和一支石笔一起拿走。石板背面有一道旧划痕，Rosa用指甲抠了抠，抠不掉。回家路上Rosa把石板贴在胸口，隔着布也能感觉到石头的凉。
  _effects: cash -0.08 (买二手石板和石笔); skill; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1921.04** `vienna-childhood-friends.hand-in-hand/glove-left-behind` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  手套是灰蓝色的，指尖磨得发亮。同桌在课间把它推到Rosa手边，说自己戴不着。放学后同桌先走了，手套落在抽屉最里面。Rosa把它卷起来塞进衣兜，回家后藏进枕头套，谁问都说没看见。
  _effects: mood 0.06; condition_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Rosa前面的大婶一直往后靠，Rosa就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Rosa的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1922.10** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Rosa被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Rosa一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Helene Weiss 2提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood -0.04999999999999999; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  错题纸被Rosa攥出了汗印。Anton Stein看了一眼，说留两次可以，先把炉灰倒了。灰桶提到后院时，风从巷口灌进来，灰扑了Rosa一脸。Anton Stein回来没催，用粉笔在黑板上把一道竖式拆成三步，让Rosa跟着写在自己的石板边上。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  _effects: flag +read_for_conductor; skill; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  _effects: flag +name_in_hiring_book; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.07_

  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

  _effects: cash -3 (stamped copies and fees); reputation -0.03; flag +appeal_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  _effects: cash -12 (拉丁文的旧课本（其中12克朗记入债务）); flag +family_sold_ring_for_school; relation friend with person-franz-lechner; education secondary_

- **1925.03** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1925.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  Rosa在操场边堵住Berta Huber，问他从哪听来的。他先说是别人说的，后来承认是自己编的，因为Rosa在课堂上答对了他答错的问题。Franz Lechner让他们各写一份说明，两份都锁进抽屉。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +confronted_rumor_source; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; knowledge “the classmate invented the rumour out of envy”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  弥撒进行到一半，Rosa忽然唱不出声，只张嘴。Maria Lechner用琴声盖过去，Berta Weiss回头看了一眼。散场后Maria Lechner把Rosa的独唱位置给了新来的男孩，说这不是惩罚，是嗓子到了年纪。Rosa把谱子折好，放进抽屉最底层。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +replaced_as_soloist; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework)_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/too-young-refused` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  “开春再来。”Emil Hofer 3说完就转身去接纸车。Rosa站在门槛外，把这句话在心里复述了一遍，弯腰捡起地上的纸边。两个赫勒落在掌心时还带着围裙的体温。回家那条街上有三家亮着灯的作坊，Rosa一家一家看过去，把位置记住。
  _effects: cash 0.2 (替作坊捡纸边得到的两个赫勒); mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; role; flag +recommendation_letter; skill_

  Rosa把一桩房租纠纷的判例背得滚瓜烂熟，Franz Lechner在课上让Rosa复述判决理由。Rosa讲到一半，Franz Lechner打断，问如果换了被告的处境会怎么判。Rosa想了很久，说可能不一样。Franz Lechner说，这才是学法律的开头，然后让全班把这个问题抄下来。问题写了满满一页。

  Rosa和Katharina Kral谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Rosa家，钥匙只在白天给。Katharina Kral说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Mitzi听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Rosa把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  家里那间屋子朝北，下午的光只够照亮桌角。Rosa把车钱塞进外套内袋，听见母亲在数硬币时嘴里念念有词。师兄说过别到处讲，Rosa就没讲，连妹妹问起也只说“够用”。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1931.12** `vienna-history-schnitzler-theatre/overhear-the-intermission` tags: history, culture, arthur_schnitzler, burgtheater, theatre
  幕间休息时，Rosa 看见Arthur Schnitzler 独自站在楼梯平台，手里捏着一张节目单。Rosa 没有上前，只听见他对剧院经理说，今晚的删改让第三幕失去了锋芒。散场后，Rosa 把节目单收进外套口袋，上面还留着铅笔划过的痕迹。
  _effects: knowledge “Burgtheater 的演出会因审查和经理安排删改台词；作者本人未必能阻止。”; relation acquaintance with person-arthur-schnitzler; cash -3 (theatre ticket); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1932.01** `state_expire:apprenticeship-contract` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick, state:apprenticeship-contract
  Rosa的「学徒契约」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:18; role_

- **1932.01** `task-vienna-writer-researcher.abandon-project/project-reworked` tags: knowledge, culture, writing, editor, library, publication, task, task:knowledge
  Rosa把第三十七页从本子上撕下来，碎纸丢进废纸篓，从第一人称重新起笔。三个月里她改成一部五十页的短稿，用挂号寄给Luise Stein，信封和稿纸花掉一点二克朗，母亲的药费是向Rosa Moser 2借了五克朗才垫上的。
  _effects: skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; cash -1.2 (挂号寄稿的纸张与邮资（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); mood 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1932.03** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/keep-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Rosa把Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Rosa没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: flag +movement_list_holder; reputation 0.03_

- **1932.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  有人喊罢工，有人喊谈判，Rosa站到前排说先动才有得谈。保守派转身去拿大衣，Gustav Winter追出去又回来，说外头雪深，别把人推到对面去。会议没散，火气没降。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1932.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Rosa用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

- **1932.10** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Rosa的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Anna Stein拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1933.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lending.coal-money` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Franz Weiss说煤店的账拖不过去，向Rosa借五克朗。Rosa从工钱袋里抽出钱，Franz Weiss在烟盒背面写了借条，说发薪日一定还；两人把烟盒压在厨房的盐罐下面。那天晚上，Rosa家的炉子只烧了半筐煤。
  _effects: cash -5 (lent five kronen for the neighbour’s coal bill); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; role; flag +loan_outstanding; knowledge “借条写在烟盒背面，压在厨房的盐罐下面。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Friedrich Kral提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Rosa把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; flag +parish_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.06** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/chalk-cross-and-rent-hike` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Lechner在Rosa的门框上画了一个白叉，说这一层要“清理”，外国人的房租从下月起加两克朗。Rosa问租约上没写这一条，Mitzi Lechner说租约是旧政府的，现在归他解释。Gustav Moser在隔壁把门开了一条缝，又轻轻关上。
  _effects: cash -4 (门上的粉笔叉（其中3.03克朗记入债务）); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +marked_with_chalk_cross; relation rival with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “the landlord marks foreign tenants with a chalk cross”_

- **1934.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.full-bag` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Rosa把一整袋面粉倒进Luise Kral的空口袋，袋口用麻绳扎了两道，说等她男人发了薪再还。Luise Kral连声道谢，把写着借煤日期的小纸片折好放回围裙。柜子里只剩一袋面粉，Rosa当晚煮汤时多加了一勺水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1934.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Friedrich Kral借给Rosa一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Rosa睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; mood -0.06000000000000005_

  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Katharina Berg帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Rosa点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org-wien-magistrat; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  _effects: cash 3 (戒酒会上的第十个签名); reputation -0.03; flag +refused_temperance_pledge; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; knowledge “the tavern extends credit to unemployed workers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1935.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Rosa没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1936.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Rosa的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1936.01** `r13-edu.sell-books::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cheap-sale-for-coal` tags: education, books, sale, cash, study, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa急等钱用，Rosa Moser只肯给两克朗，说这些书明年就改版。Rosa咬着牙卖了，回家路上用那点钱买了面粉和煤，剩下的塞进母亲的围裙口袋。那天晚上家里生了火，Rosa却觉得比平时冷。母亲把围裙口袋缝了两针，没问钱是哪来的。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉的课本); food_

- **1936.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Rosa靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.1克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1936.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Rosa手里。Mitzi Moser的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Emil Hofer隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Mitzi Moser再见到Rosa时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Rosa站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06000000000000001; mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  _effects: role; reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; cash -1 (签名纸、墨水和面包店门口的租金（其中1克朗记入债务）); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分开，窗口职员会当面数签名”; flag +petition_filed_

  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Mitzi Moser把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Rosa打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Rosa看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Rosa把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1937.05** `r14-soc.con-artist::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/patent-that-did-not-exist` tags: con_artist, fraud, money, trust, crime, vienna, urban, crisis
  Katharina Weiss把一张盖着红章的专利副本铺在桌上，说只要二十克朗入股，明年就能在每条街上装一台自动售烟机。Rosa数钱时，Katharina Weiss一直用手帕擦杯子。三个月后，那个地址只剩一间空屋和半张撕掉的红章。巡警来做笔录，问了三遍“红章是什么颜色”，Rosa答了三遍。
  _effects: cash -20 (不存在的专利号（其中17克朗记入债务）); mood -0.2899999999999999; condition; flag +conned_by_partner; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; knowledge “the patent office has no record of the machine”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_

  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

  Rosa在家把一锅扁豆汤分出一半，让Anton端回去给生病的老母亲。Anton第二天还回一只空锅，锅底放着一小块猪油。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.12** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Rosa把照片铁盒交给Katharina Moser 2抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.05 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  Rosa在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Rosa的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。

  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1939.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.church-bell` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001037:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04_

  _effects: cash -20 (账单上的最后一行); condition; flag +medical_debt; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; knowledge “nursing and heating are billed separately from the operation”_

- **1943.11** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty

  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Anton Huber 3点了牛奶咖啡，Rosa要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (coffee and a small gift for the matchmaker); role; flag +walked_out_together; knowledge “对方在洗衣房做工，母亲长期病着。”_

  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Lena在投票前站起来，说今年不选了，让Mitzi Kral连任，条件是账目每月贴在门口。Mitzi Kral答应得很快。散会后，几个老团员拍Lena的肩，说“你倒像当家的”。第二个月，账目真的贴在了门口，第一页的墨迹被雨淋得发花。第一页是空白的，第二页才开始有字。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +withdrew_from_club_election; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05000000000000002 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (雾里的冰裂缝); mood 0.050000000000000044; condition; flag +spec14_mountain_rescued; knowledge “the snow bridge collapsed under a layer of fresh drift”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.08** `statistical_infection`
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1947.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1947.10** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.bruised-apples` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  她蹲在菜市场湿漉漉的石板地上挑苹果，Anna Stein从旁边经过，说这种碰伤的煮出来最甜。她回家把苹果切成块，锅里水刚开就下了糖精片，满屋子都是煮水果的酸香。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1947.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Lena从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1948.01** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.listen-coins` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  她本来只是去面包店买盐，被琴声绊住了脚。猴子蹲在帽沿上啃一颗栗子，风琴师脱下礼帽扇风，露出汗湿的额角。曲子完了，她数出两枚铜板放进帽子，听见身后有人吹口哨。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1948.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Lena听Grete Stein 3报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Lena穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1948.07** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, injury
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Lena一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Rosa Kraus送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); health -0.02999999999999997 (crowded sleeping space); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.11** `r3-crime.vigilante-justice::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/arrested-with-the-crowd` tags: crime, vigilante, violence, village, law, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Berg没有先抓Grete Stein 3，而是让所有人靠墙站好。Anna Weiss说“我们抓的是贼”，Katharina Berg说“我抓的是打人的”。Lena被记下名字，罚了八克朗。走出警局时，Grete Stein 3正坐在台阶上，手里捧着一杯水，没有人看他。
  _effects: cash -8 (磨坊后面立着一根新木桩); condition; flag +arrested_after_vigilante_justice; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; knowledge “the constable charged the crowd, not the suspect”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1948.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Lena缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.04999999999999999 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008327_

- **1949.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Lena看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001680:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  Lena把空煤筐拎到隔壁门口，说只借一筐，等手头松了就还。Berta Weiss 2看了看筐底的锈，进屋铲了半筐，又从灶边拿了两块引火的柴。Lena道谢时，Berta Weiss 2说这条走廊里借东西要记着，不是记在纸上。
  _effects: food; condition; flag +borrowed_coal_from_neighbour; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1949.06** `p9-work-transport.loading-yard/loading-yard-two-men` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, loading, freight
  Lena和Katharina 2一人抓一个箱角，踩着结冰的跳板往上挪。箱角裂口里漏出刨花，Lena腰上一沉，像被什么东西揪了一下，两人还是把箱子顶上了敞车。Grete Stein 3在下面拍手，说这才叫干活；Lena扶着车皮喘气，知道明天弯不下腰。
  _effects: health -0.06 (抬重箱时闪了腰); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001543:child_

- **1950.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/soup-and-grave` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  饭桌摆在圣器室旁边，椅子不够，两人挨着坐。Anton说到欠账时声音低下去，Lena没有接话，只把盐罐递过去；出门时天已经擦黑，墓地的铁门还开着。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (墓前的蜡烛); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1950.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Luise Kral说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Luise Kral陪着Lena去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-luise-kral; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1950.07** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.share-spinach` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  两人同时伸手，又同时缩回去。Friedrich Bauer 2说自己本来想买回去做馅，Lena说那正好一人一半。摊主用草绳把菜捆好，嘴里嘟囔着收摊了收摊了，把剩下的零钱扒拉进木匣。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Lena把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Mitzi Huber站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Lena手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146_

  Lena没有让Luise Kral进门，只把救济站的地址写在烟盒背面，又画了怎么绕过岗亭。Luise Kral把烟盒攥在手里，鞠了一躬。第二天，走廊的墙上多了一张用铅笔写的谢谢。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +directed_refugee_to_relief; knowledge “the relief office opens at seven and does not ask for a resi”_

  Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)愿意把评语写足，说“勤勉、可靠、熟悉账目”，但要求Lena留到年底，把新来的Mitzi Bauer带到能单独上柜台为止。证明先不写，只给了一张临时字条。Lena拿着字条去答复新东家，对方说位置可以等到圣灵降临节。他回到柜台，把学徒的账本又翻了出来。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill; flag +delayed_reference_

  Lena的剪刀钝得咬布，一剪下去袖口走了斜线。她索性把两只袖子都裁短，袖边用灰线锁了一圈。锁边时线太短，接到第三段才够，右手食指被针顶出个红印，改完像件旧式的短褂。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Lena从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  _effects: cash -4 (天花板上爬满了虫子); health -0.16000000000000003 (天花板上爬满了虫子); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; knowledge “withdrawal can bring hallucinations and a fight against the ”_

  到了警局，失窃清单上忽然多出一只银怀表，Berta Huber 3说是在Lena的篮子底下找到的。Lena说那篮子从早上就没离手，Rosa 2低头擦柜台，Anna Winter被叫去另一间屋子。值班的人把“盗窃”改成“加重盗窃”，让她按手印。
  _effects: cash -6 (后巷里数面包的手); condition; flag +overcharged_after_petty_theft; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; knowledge “the silver watch was added to the list after the arrest”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

  Lena输了五票，要求把票重新数一遍。数到第二遍还是五票，Grete Stein 3把铁盒盖上，说“再数就难看了”。Lena说账本也得查，后厅一下子安静下来。那晚的合唱没有唱成。散会后，Grete Stein 3把铁盒抱在怀里，锁进酒馆老板的柜子。铁盒被放回柜子，账本却留在了桌上。
  _effects: reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +disputed_club_election; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1954.11** `p9-work-transport.coupling-injury/coupling-injury-infirmary` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, shunting, injury
  Mitzi Bauer先跑过来，把Lena半扶半架到值班室旁的小医务室。脚踝肿成一块，铁路按事故保险付了药膏和包扎的钱，Grete Stein 3在事故单上写了“调车受伤”，随后通知本月起把他调离调车组，去看仓库的收发簿。
  _effects: health -0.08999999999999997 (缓冲器撞伤小腿与脚踝); cash 0.6 (事故保险报销的药费); flag +moved_off_shunting_crew_

  旧货商把床架往秤上一放，报了个数。工具他留了一套最顺手的，用旧布裹了三层。领事馆的窗户开得很高，队伍里有人咳嗽，有人把文件摊在膝盖上又看一遍。
  _effects: cash -8 (补齐船票、护照手续费与体检单费用，已扣卖家具所得); mood 0.06000000000000005; condition; knowledge “去美国的船票加手续费一共一百多克朗，统舱最便宜”_

- **1955.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Lena按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

  Lena刚开口，Franz Hofer就把报纸举高，说这张桌子有人。Lena端着杯子在门口站了一会儿，回去把椅子放正；第二天同一时辰，Franz Hofer没有来，桌上留着一圈没擦干的杯印。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +rebuffed_while_seeking_friend; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001337:child_

- **1956.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把Anna Stein和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Lena把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1956.11** `r2-leisure.coachman-night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/back-before-dawn` tags: domestic_service, coachman, horse, night, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena在四点前把枣红马牵回马厩，马蹄上的麻布已经磨破。Helene 2从草堆里坐起来，说老爷六点要用车。Lena把银币塞进他手里，又替他把草叉放回原处。天亮时，马槽里的水还是温的。
  _effects: cash 2 (马厩里的两点钟); flag +secret_night_ride; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004799:child; knowledge “the stableboy will cover a night ride for a silver coin”_

- **1956.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena看Berta Bauer 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Berta Bauer 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Lena门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Lena打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; food_

- **1957.01** `p9-work-career.night-course/p9-work-career.night-course.review` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, night_school, bookkeeping
  Lena和Helene 2的书都缺页，两人把笔记摊在咖啡馆后桌，交替着抄，谁先到谁先占座位，一杯咖啡续到打烊。结课前一夜复习到十一点，Josef Weiss第二天发卷子时扫了一眼他们的笔记，说抄得比听讲还整齐。Lena考了七十八分，结业证照发，只是名次靠后。
  _effects: cash -6 (夜校学费); cash -1 (咖啡馆里续杯与夜点); skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004799:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161_

  铜牌上的门牌号在六区一条窄街上，楼梯间的墙皮起了鼓包，二楼有人在拉手风琴。Lena牵着狗沿街走，狗时走时停，鼻子贴着一排门槛嗅过去。门房老头接过绳子时说了句这狗他认得，是四楼裁缝家的。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  店主来收煤气灯，Lena把铜灯擦净递过去，Josef在门外数着散场的人。有人回头问Lena下次站哪边，Lena说等椅子摆回原样再想。夜里风把纸板吹倒了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.opened` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  信封没封牢，Lena在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000138; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

- **1958.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-sent` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban

  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”_

- **1958.02** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Friedrich Huber问是谁动的。Friedrich Huber说是Helene Lechner嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  小球落进黑格，又弹起来，最后停在绿色的零上。Helene 2把Lena的筹码收走，动作轻得像收一副餐具。Franz Hofer在旁边说，早就该走。Lena摸遍口袋，只找到一张电车票。Grete Stein 3在门口等着，手里拿着上个月的欠条。
  _effects: cash -25 (轮盘停在零); condition; flag +lost_everything_on_zero; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001337:child; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1958.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/refuses_grudge` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Ferdinand Moser敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Lena那天刚和Ferdinand Moser为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Ferdinand Moser抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Ferdinand Moser低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

  Katharina Berg翻完材料，问Lena一个月挣多少、住几间房、有没有人帮忙看孩子。Lena把工资单和房东的证明一起推过去，Otto Kral在门口补充说，孩子放学后可以去她家。Katharina Berg在“适宜”一栏打了勾，让Lena下个月来领正式文书。
  _effects: flag +guardianship_granted; role; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the court grants guardianship but reviews the household in a”_

  Rosa Huber 2把登记簿摊在门房的小桌上，让Lena写下姓名和住址。Lena写完后，Rosa Huber 2在名字旁边画了一个小十字，说下周去给孤老送煤。本子上前面几行，墨迹已经褪成了褐色。
  _effects: cash -1 (慈善会登记簿上的空行); reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +joined_charity_visiting_round; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

  _effects: cash -35 (first television instalment); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +television_at_home; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-171; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-171_

  “居留章呢？”Katharina Berg用拇指按住身份证上的旧地址。Lena说上个月刚搬，Katharina Berg说搬了就该去换章。Berta Huber 3从岗亭里探出头，让把人带回去。拘留室的墙上有一排钉子，挂过帽子，也挂过手铐。
  _effects: cash -3 (路灯下翻出的口袋内衬); condition; flag +detained_for_missing_residence_stamp; role; knowledge “the residence stamp must match the current address”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation employer with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000146:child_

  _effects: cash 3 (只叫老师傅回去); flag +signed_a_no_union_pledge; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003229:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162; knowledge “the firm offers separate return only to skilled men who sign”; employment Werkzeugmacher @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -4 (三楼走廊里的煤油味); health -0.14 (三楼走廊里的煤油味); reputation 0.10000000000000003; condition; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002806:child; knowledge “the neighbour would not leave the iron box behind”_

  Mitzi Weiss用拇指甲刮着一件领子的边线，说这儿跳了针。Lena把煤油灯往前推了推，让他凑近看，说线是细了点，活没少做。Anna Stein倚在门框上没走，屋里一时只有尺子碰桌面的响。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000146:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1961.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Lena在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Lena的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  签名纸在电车上被挤掉一角，最后十个名字不见了。她站在终点站找了一刻钟，只在座位下找到一张被踩脏的纸；Helene Hofer说“算了”，她还是把剩下的名字重新抄了一遍。她把重抄的名单交给Helene Hofer，让对方再核一遍。
  _effects: flag +petition_signatures_lost; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001496:child; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸和电车票); health -0.010000000000000009 (在终点站找纸); knowledge “签名纸要夹在硬纸板里，电车挤掉的一角找不回来”_

- **1961.02** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Lena把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Alois Moser 2递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-134; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-134_

  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; cash -2 (settlement for the apples); flag +accused_in_store_

  Lena把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Anton的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Lena在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。

  _effects: cash -10 (新城区门牌下的招租纸); flag +lodging_key_money_paid; knowledge “the previous lodger’s furniture was held for key money”_

  _effects: flag +ordinary_sad_year_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Anna Moser在绳子那头喊。Anton踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Anna Moser数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Anton把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Anna Moser把Anton拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Anton把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Hofer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt门口，Anton蹲下把两包并排，按Anna Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1919.03** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Anton每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Ferdinand Lechner擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Anton把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Ferdinand Lechner看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1919.06** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Anton弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Anton把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Josefine Hofer把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Anton每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood -0.06_

  _effects: reputation -0.05; condition; flag +police_searched_attic; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the police searched the attic but did not move the coal box”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  Anton在人群外站到腿发酸，只隔着跳舞的人看了Helene Weiss 3几眼。散场时Mitzi Weiss裹着围巾凑过来，Anton低声托Mitzi Weiss去打听Helene Weiss 3的门第和姓氏，自己先出了酒馆，在冷风里等回话；一整晚没敢上前，鞋底粘着酒馆地板的糖渍。

  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

  Anton数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Anton在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Helene Bauer道了别，约好下回还来排队。

  _effects: cash -0.3 (还价后买下旧杯子); skill_

  _effects: skill; cash -0.02 (向管理员缴纳的借书押金); mood 0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  军需处先领走了奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林和泻盐。Anton用一双旧靴子换了两支吗啡，药剂师把安瓿裹在报纸里，叮嘱别在街上打开。他把安瓿藏进帽衬，过检查站时低着头。
  _effects: cash -20 (boots and cash traded for two morphine ampoules); health 0.020000000000000018 (pain relief from a black-market ampoule); role; flag +traded_for_morphine; knowledge “军需处先领走奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林；旧靴子可以换两支吗啡。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  Anton在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Anna Stein 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
