- **1944.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Anton在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Anton拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Anton Bauer找到Anton时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001309:child_

- **1944.09** `p9-history-medical-daily.sanitation-inspection/sign-only-own-household` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  表格上要写每户人数和厕所使用情况，Anton念到“代签人负责”时停住了。Grete Stein 3等了一会儿，自己按了个手印；巡查员走后，楼梯口有人说Anton认得字反倒更精。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; knowledge “卫生巡查表上每户只能签自己那一栏，代签要负连带责任”_

- **1945.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Anna Stein 2在几天后找到Anton，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; household; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006993_

- **1945.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.listen-only` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Anton坐在后厅角落的啤酒箱上听完整场排练，Mitzi Winter中途走过来问要不要在名册上签字，Anton说再想想。散场时人们三三两两去前厅续杯，Anton把空杯子放回柜台，顺着酒馆后门的巷子走回家，嘴里还留着半段旋律；这一晚没花钱，也和Mitzi Winter多说了两句话。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.02** `p9-history-union-political.reform-meeting/reform-study-committee` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  会议开到最后，只通过成立一个研究委员会，说三个月后交报告。散会时Anton和Berta Stein在门口商量下一步，有人提议先发动街区签名，把工价和房租的数字附在报告后面。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Anton躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Anton去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1946.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/collected-forty-kroner` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Franz Hofer把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Anton把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001337:child; organization_funds_

- **1946.08** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  床架从楼梯上拖下来时油漆蹭掉了好几块，Anton站在旁边没作声。收破烂的走后，屋里空得回声都变大了，Anton抱着铁盒出门，只把门锁好，钥匙从看门人手里交出去。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002071:child_

- **1946.12** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-and-mother-weak` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth
  孩子出生后取名Hermann Bauer，Anna Stein 2一直出血。Anton跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Anna Stein 2落下产后虚弱的毛病，Anton抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; cash -18 (doctor and medicine)_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Anton没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005956_

- **1947.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/crowd-tears-notices` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton刚把告示刷上墙，就有人围过来说这是拿疫病吓人。一个卖菜的女人伸手把纸撕下来，浆糊蹭了Anton一脸。Grete 2把Anton拉到一边，说这条街上个月才埋了两个孩子。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (街角防疫告示的浆糊); reputation -0.03; flag +public_health_notice_torn_down; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001922:child; knowledge “the market crowd blames the notice for frightening customers”_

- **1947.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Anton按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1947.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Lena在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.12** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Friedrich Lechner在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Lechner在门后放了一根木棍，Anton把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1948.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/church-hall` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  教堂侧厅的长凳被挪到墙边，旧锅架在砖灶上，Josef 蹲着添柴，Anton 把补好的账页贴在门板。第一锅汤滚起来时，排队的人从门口一直站到院里。
  _effects: cash -4 (垫付首日面粉和柴火); food; reputation 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1948.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.cotton-in-ears` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton忍到天亮，第二天去药房买了五分钱的棉花，撕成两小团塞在耳里。夜里的进行曲还剩一点闷响，翻个身也就过去了。此后在楼梯间遇见Helene Hofer只是点头，再没有进过那家的门。
  _effects: cash -0.05 (在药房买棉花塞耳朵); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001496:child_

- **1948.05** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.bruised-apples` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  她蹲在菜市场湿漉漉的石板地上挑苹果，Josef Lechner从旁边经过，说这种碰伤的煮出来最甜。她回家把苹果切成块，锅里水刚开就下了糖精片，满屋子都是煮水果的酸香。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果); food_

- **1948.07** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.wring-blanket` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  毯子吸饱了水，沉得像一条死狗。Grete Lechner说这毯子是她婆婆的嫁妆，一年只洗一回。Anton咬着牙拧到最后一圈，水从毯缝里挤出来，在脚边汇成一小片温热。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (拧厚毯子把手勒得发酸); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001352:child_

- **1948.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Anton手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000438:child_

- **1949.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Friedrich Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Friedrich Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1949.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Friedrich Lechner在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Friedrich Lechner把煤钱推过来，Anton接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.plan-wedding::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wedding-debt` tags: task, family, marriage, wedding, household, debt, vienna, urban
  Anton不肯让Friedrich Lechner家看轻，借了钱把婚宴摆到四桌，还请了乐队。散席后，账房把欠条一张张摊开，Friedrich Lechner的姑母当场沉了脸。新婚第一夜，两人在灯下算到后半夜，喜烛烧完了一半。
  _effects: cash -20 (婚宴要摆几桌); condition; flag +wedding_paid_on_credit; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132; knowledge “the wedding debt will take a year to clear”; household_

- **1949.03** `r1-cul.tourism-trip::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/overheard-innkeeper` tags: culture, travel, leisure, companionship, weather, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton半夜被隔壁的争吵吵醒，Katharina 2也醒着。两人披着外套坐在楼梯口，听见旅馆老板在跟一个男人算账。Anton Stein后来端来两杯热酒，什么也没解释；Katharina 2说，这趟旅行至少带回一个故事。
  _effects: cash -2 (山间旅馆的钥匙); mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +overheard_innkeeper_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; knowledge “the innkeeper owes money to a man from the valley”_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:university-dropout` tags: university-dropout, education, dropout, stigma, state, state_tick, state:university-dropout
  Anton的「大学退学」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove university-dropout state:world-vienna-1912:43; flag -university_dropout_

- **1950.01** `p9-work-career.security-or-independence/p9-work-career.security-or-independence.salary` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, independence, security
  Anton在契约上签了字，月薪八十五克朗，年底另有补贴。他把攒下的三十克朗原封不动收进铁盒，租门面的念头压到抽屉最底层。Friedrich Lechner数着月份算，说这样下一年冬天可以添一床羊毛毯。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)把办公室钥匙串上他那把，说试用三个月。
  _effects: cash 85 (固定职位首月薪金); employment 固定办事员 @ org-sdap; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +gave_up_own_sign; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

- **1950.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton数出八赫勒交给Maria Kraus，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Maria Kraus说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Anton盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000227:child; flag +funeral_society_member_

  Anton把通知念给Josef Lechner听，Josef Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Anton去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.belt-patch` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Anton拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (锥子扎伤拇指根); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008989_

- **1951.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Anton的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Anton被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.030000000000000027 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”_

- **1951.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Anton听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Rosa Stein 2扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999994 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1951.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Helene Weiss 2问是谁动的。Helene Weiss 2说是Friedrich Huber嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.join-choir::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/voice-rejected` tags: culture, choir, music, community, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Franz Lechner听完，说音准还行，但声音太薄，撑不起礼拜天。Anton问能不能先站在最后一排，Franz Lechner说等下次有空位。回家的路上，Anton在无人的巷子里把那首歌唱完。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +choir_audition_rejected_

- **1952.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Rosa 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Rosa 2陪着Anton去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002959:child; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1952.10** `r3-ind.company-housing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-flat-quietly` tags: work, housing, company_town, eviction, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton没有去争退房，而是让Franz Huber 2在考勤簿上把自己的名字多留了一个月，工钱照扣，房子照住。Katharina Berg来查过两次，Franz Huber 2都说人在夜班。Anton每天天亮才回家，避开邻居的视线，钥匙用绳子挂在脖子上。
  _effects: cash -2 (公司宿舍的钥匙); flag +kept_company_housing_after_dismissal; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000373:child; knowledge “the foreman is covering an absent name on the attendance boo”_

  Anton和Anton Huber 3买了影戏棚最便宜的边座，边座和两粒糖一共五十赫勒。一刻钟的活动影戏里火车朝观众冲过来，棚里有人叫出了声。散场后两人还站在门口看下一场的招牌，Anton把剩下几个铜板在口袋里攥得发热，又买了两粒糖，一人一粒含回家。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (影戏棚边座和两粒糖); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1953.09** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Helene Weiss 2来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Anton切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food_

- **1953.09** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.argument` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  星期天中午，Anton和Friedrich Lechner在出租屋的厨房桌边分面包，话题从房租转到Anton母亲上个月的药钱。Friedrich Lechner把刀放下，说这个家不能总先填那边的窟窿。面包凉了，汤也没再热。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1954.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, injury
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Anton一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Gustav Winter 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); health -0.030000000000000027 (crowded sleeping space); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1954.01** `p10-migration-tasks.emigrate/emigrate-sell-and-sail` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, emigration, passport
  的里雅斯特的码头有鱼腥味。检票员用钳子在票角夹了个圆孔，他捏着那只箱子，箱底还粘着维也纳的煤灰。邻座的老妇人分给他半块面包，他没说话，点了点头。
  _effects: cash -8 (补齐船票、护照手续费与体检单费用，已扣卖家具所得); mood 0.06000000000000005; condition; knowledge “去美国的船票加手续费一共一百多克朗，统舱最便宜”_

- **1954.07** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.wring-blanket` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  两个人拧毯子的时候，Gustav Winter 2在旁边递肥皂水，说你们俩力气倒合得上。毯子抬上绳子时还在滴水，Anton甩了甩手，看见自己掌纹里嵌着一道一道的布纹。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (拧厚毯子把手勒得发酸); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1954.09** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Anton Bauer 2在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1954.12** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton数出八赫勒交给Berta Huber 3，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Berta Huber 3说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Anton盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

  Mitzi Kral翻到报名纸背面，说还缺一张出生证明和一双完整的鞋。Anton低头看Hermann Bauer脚上那双露脚趾的鞋，说下周补上；Mitzi Kral把纸推回来，说名额只留到星期五。回家的路上，Hermann Bauer一直问学校是什么样子。
  _effects: cash -1 (书包带系紧一点); mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +school_enrolment_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1955.07** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-bed-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  顶楼那间屋里有三张床，靠墙一张空着，床板上留着上一个住客的煤灰印。Anton把铺盖卷放下，试了试窗上的插销，外面是通风井。Ferdinand Huber帮着把一只小箱子搁在床头，说这地方不要指望晒到太阳。
  _effects: cash -3 (先付一张床位的月租); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

  护照窗口的玻璃上有一道裂纹，Katharina Berg把他的材料从裂纹下面推回来，说缺少出生证明。他跑了两趟教堂、一趟市政厅，最后在旧箱底找到一张洗礼纸；再回到窗口时，队伍已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -12 (护照费、出生证明补办费和车票); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000373:child; unemployment (辞掉工作准备离境); knowledge “护照需要出生证明，洗礼纸可以作为旧式证明”_

- **1956.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Maria Lechner抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Maria Lechner用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `task-vienna-writer-researcher.library-research/library-closed` tags: knowledge, culture, writing, editor, library, publication, task, task:knowledge
  档案室比阅览室更早关门。Anton刚写明要调阅的登记号，管钥匙的人已经挂上了铁钩。他只能凭记忆把脚注补个大概，用半截铅笔在信封背面写下“待核”两个字，走出图书馆时天已全黑。
  _effects: cash -0.2 (次日再跑一趟所需的电车票); mood -0.040000000000000036; skill_

- **1957.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock, injury
  多瑙河码头的雾还没散，工头捏着名单念了Anton的名字。煤灰落在汗湿的脖颈上，Friedrich Lechner在铁门边看着，说这活儿一天顶三天，可肩膀得疼上一个礼拜。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱); health -0.050000000000000044 (扛煤筐磨破肩膀); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-171_

- **1958.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.listen-only` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Anton坐在后厅角落的啤酒箱上听完整场排练，Helene Bauer中途走过来问要不要在名册上签字，Anton说再想想。散场时人们三三两两去前厅续杯，Anton把空杯子放回柜台，顺着酒馆后门的巷子走回家，嘴里还留着半段旋律；这一晚没花钱，也和Helene Bauer多说了两句话。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000707:child_

  Anton把Die Fackel的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Anton没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: flag +movement_list_holder; reputation 0.03_

- **1959.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.deposit-refused` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  “十克朗押金，先交后干。”Grete Stein 3说得很平。Anton把两只口袋翻给人家看，柜台上只落下一张皱车票。工头收回话头，他也不再问，提着帽子走出栅栏。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +seeking_work; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160_

- **1959.04** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Lena在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  念到房子一段，Lena抬手说不同意，理由是他这些年也往家里寄过钱。公证人Anton把文件收回抽屉，说明需要双方再谈一次，约到下周三。走出门时Anton先说了话，对方没有回，两人在石阶上一左一右地走开。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1960.08** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-damaged-deducted` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor, injury
  柜脚磕在石阶边缘那一声，Helene Bauer在楼上就听见了。下来摸着裂缝，说这柜子跟了十几年，你们抬东西也太毛躁。Anton把手在裤子上蹭了蹭，血印子蹭出一条。
  _effects: cash 1.2 (搬家磕坏柜脚被扣工钱); health -0.020000000000000018 (搬柜子时手背划伤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-157_

- **1961.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/meet-at-church-door` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  两人谁也没提柜子，饭吃得客气而生分。临走时Anton提议：和不和好另说，每年忌日就在教堂门口见一面，站十分钟也好。Lena想了一下，说行，但别指望他进屋。Anton把这话记在心上，回去路上把没送出去的半包烟丝塞回口袋。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +truce_with_sibling_

  介绍所里那个Berta Winter 3说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Anton走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

- **1962.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.read-all` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Anton把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Anton折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

- **1962.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.repair-shop` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Anton用围裙裹着钟走到钟表铺，铺子里的老头把钟翻过来看了看，说要换发条，连工带料一克朗二十赫勒。她算了算这星期的菜钱，把钟又裹回围裙里提回家，搁在柜上，没再提修的事。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999907_

- **1962.11** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Die Fackel的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org-wien-magistrat; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-197; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-197; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-202_

  Anton缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  前面还有七八个人时，Rosa 2已经直起腰把锅盖扣上了。队伍没散，有人骂了一句，有人蹲下把罐子收进布袋。Anton走到巷口，Gustav Bauer从后面赶上来，罐子还温着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007284:child_

  Anton夹着一叠印着候选人头像的传单，从巷口走到巷尾。有人在围裙上擦手，问候选人是不是也住这样的楼。Anton答不上来，只把传单往门缝里又塞了一张。天黑时，口袋里只剩三张。

  Anton没有买电视，晚饭后照旧打开收音机。Helene Bauer说屏幕上的东西不过是会动的报纸；两人在厨房里听完新闻，又讨论了一会儿电价。
  _effects: cash -1 (radio licence); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000707:child; flag +radio_evenings_

  Anton走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个); health -0.010000000000000009 (在风口里走了一段，脸被吹得发红)_

  _effects: cash -2 (记者证上的照片); flag +press_card_issued; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; knowledge “a lost press card will not be replaced”_

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

  报亭后面的墙根下，Josef Lechner把咬了一半的糖递过去，换回一张火车头画片。糖纸被风吹到摊主的脚边。Gustav Berg弯腰捡起来，把画片从Josef Lechner手里抽走，说先把地扫了。Josef Lechner扫到墙根，Gustav Berg才把画片还回来。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1928.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Berta Weiss家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Josef Lechner递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Berta Weiss端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Josef Lechner把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1928.02** `r1-fam.baptism-confirmation::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::confirmation-reform/confirmation.refused` tags: family, faith, confirmation, godparent, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Stein加入社会民主党以后，不肯再让孩子进教堂。Anton Moser 2来家里两趟，说没有坚振证明，以后找工作要吃亏；Anna Stein把工会的报纸铺在桌上，说新社会不看这个。Josef Lechner在门后听着，把蜡烛从抽屉里拿出来，又放回去。
  _effects: flag +confirmation_refused_by_family; role; knowledge “有些工作要看坚振证明。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1929.01** `r14-edu.music-lessons::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/accepted-as-pupil` tags: education, arts_training, music, craft, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Anton Stein让Josef Lechner把手放在琴键上，先不按键，只听节拍器。Josef Lechner坐了十分钟，手心全是汗。Anton Stein说手型不对，但耳朵可以教。从那天起，Josef Lechner每周三放学后去琴房，练到天黑，回家的路上手指还在桌板上敲。每月学费三克朗。
  _effects: cash -3 (琴房里漏风的窗); role; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1929.05** `r1-child.pencil-betrayal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-police-school/denounced-to-police` tags: friendship, betrayal, theft, school, shame, reform, medical, schooling
  新校规说偷窃要记入品行册，还要通知警察。Berta Bauer的父亲是巡警，当天就来了学校。Berta Bauer在办公室里改口，说Josef Lechner逼Berta Bauer偷的。Josef Lechner看着窗外的警帽，一句话也没说。
  _effects: reputation -0.14; flag +denounced_to_police_by_friend; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000848:child_

- **1929.09** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  Josef Lechner拼到一半停住，Anton Stein的尺子落在桌角上。放学后楼梯间只剩Josef Lechner一个人，把“Notwendigkeit”抄了二十遍，墨水瓶见了底。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1930.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Josef Lechner把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Josef Lechner踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Josef Lechner单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; skill_

- **1930.02** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef Lechner被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Josef Lechner一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Ferdinand Kraus提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000430:child; mood -0.04999999999999999; condition_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/tears-on-the-page` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Grete Lechner把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Josef Lechner把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Josef Lechner只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Grete Lechner在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001352:child; condition_

- **1932.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/first-recital` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef Lechner在期末演奏会上拉完一首短曲，弓毛断了两根，最后一个音还是稳住了。Franz Lechner在台下点头，师母把手套摘下来鼓掌。回家后，Josef Lechner把琴擦了三遍，才发现肩托把脖子磨出一道红印，碰一下就疼。Josef Lechner没有告诉任何人。琴盒没关。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +first_recital; skill_

- **1932.07** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Josef Lechner在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Anton Moser 2跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Josef Lechner去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; education apprenticeship_

- **1932.11** `r3-teen.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/homesick` tags: education, boarding_school, dormitory, homesickness, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef Lechner在盥洗室哭了两次，第三次被Franz Lechner看见。Franz Lechner没有安慰，只让她写一张明信片回家，地址写工整。明信片寄出十天后才到，母亲的回信只有一行字：好好念。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +homesick_at_boarding_school; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner_

- **1933.01** `r13-edu.buy-books::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/second-volume-only` tags: education, books, purchase, study, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef Lechner攒够钱去买，那套书已经被人买走。Josef Lechner在摊上翻了半天，只找到第二卷。回家后，Josef Lechner照着第二卷的目录，把第一卷的章节猜着抄了一遍，抄错的地方用红笔标出，第二天拿去问老师。他没有回答，只让他自己补。
  _effects: cash -4 (旧书摊上的第三卷); mood -0.050000000000000044; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1933.02** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef Berg看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Anna Stein求了半天，Josef Berg答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Anna Lechner干了三天就走了，Josef Lechner留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Josef Berg才让他碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001037:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Josef Lechner没有寄信，把信塞进Anton Berg常去的书店，夹在一本旧书里。三个月后，书被卖掉，信也不见了；Franz Lechner说那家书店上个月清过仓，旧书论斤卖。Josef Lechner去废纸站找了一下午，只找到半张写着我不敢寄的纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  毕业钟敲过三下，Franz Lechner把成绩单按在讲台上，说Josef Lechner还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Josef Lechner签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

  Josef Lechner每晚给Berta Lechner讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Josef Lechner的名次掉了十几位，Anton Moser 2在家长会上点名。回家后，Josef Lechner看见Berta Lechner趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +neglected_own_studies_

  Josef Lechner用Ferdinand Kraus借的钢笔写完最后一题，笔尖在纸上划出一道细缝。放榜那天，Franz Lechner把Josef Lechner的名字念了两遍，Ferdinand Kraus站在人群后面，先鼓掌，再低头看自己的鞋。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; role; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; education secondary_

  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Josef Lechner留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。

  _effects: flag +vaccination_reaction_mild; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +distributed_political_pamphlet; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000430:child; organization_membership; knowledge “workers accept the pamphlets at the factory gate”_

  _effects: cash -2 (换乘通道里的那只手); condition; flag +abandoned_by_pickpocket_ring; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; knowledge “the ring denies knowing arrested children”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Josef Lechner等到天黑，Anna Stein 2一直在改别的班的卷子，头也没抬。Josef Lechner把问题写在纸条上压在墨水瓶下，第二天发现纸条还在原处，只是背面多了一行字：“先看第三章。”Josef Lechner按纸条翻到第三章，发现那页夹着一张旧的辅助线图，图上的线画得很轻。

  人群往外走时，Josef Lechner把记满数字的那几页撕下来，塞进袜筒。巡警在门口逐个看包，翻到Josef Lechner的笔记本时，只看见半页抄来的诗。走出礼堂，Josef Lechner的后背已经湿透。
  _effects: flag +hid_lecture_notes_from_police; knowledge “the water statistics were copied out before the hall was cle”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1937.04** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef Lechner对Gustav Winter 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Gustav Winter 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Josef Lechner的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

  Josef Lechner把琴谱翻到最后一页，琴弦突然断了一根，声音像鞭子。Anton Stein上台把琴盖合上，说今天就到这里。Josef Lechner蹲下去捡断弦，手指被钢丝划破。Emil Winter在幕布后面看着，没有出来。修琴八克朗，Josef Lechner分了三个月还。

- **1939.01** `r13-edu.exam-retake::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-school/same-step-skipped` tags: education, exam, retake, matriculation, persistence, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Lechner又栽在同一道题上，分数只多了两分。Franz Huber 2把卷子摊开，说Josef Lechner不是不会，是每次都在同一个步骤跳步。Josef Lechner把那张卷子贴在床头，每天睡前看一遍，看到后来连错在哪一行都记得。下一次补考，Josef Lechner在那一步停了很久。笔尖顿住。
  _effects: cash -2 (补考名单上的名字); mood -0.020000000000000018; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1939.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef Lechner喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Gustav Winter在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

  Berta Lechner没有等到毕业，跟着同乡去了铁路工地上工。走的那天，Josef Lechner把攒下的两克朗塞进Berta Lechner的口袋，Berta Lechner没有推辞。火车开走后，Josef Lechner在站台上站了很久，手里还拿着那本没讲完的算术书。
  _effects: cash 2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_left_school; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001187:child_

- **1940.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.hired` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Josef Lechner在保险公司写字间的小桌上抄完一页德文书信，又算出利息表上最后一行，速记只漏了两个词。Katharina Moser 2把卷子翻过来看背面，说手是稳的。月底他领到七十克朗，扣掉十五克朗保证金，签字画押，坐进靠窗那把椅子，桌上有一块吸墨纸和一支借来的笔。
  _effects: cash 55 (首月工钱扣除保证金后实发); skill; employment 保险写字间试用办事员 @ org-volksschule-leopoldstadt; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  Josef Lechner的中学证书丢在搬家途中，Berta Huber 3说没有原件不能注册。Emil Huber 2带Josef Lechner去原校开证明，教务处的档案被水泡过，字迹模糊。两人在档案室翻了半天，找到一张点名册；Berta Huber 3最后收下点名册的抄件，说只此一次。
  _effects: cash -1 (预科班的最后一个名额); flag +admitted_with_certificate_copy; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94; knowledge “the original school records were damaged by water”; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1941.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Josef Lechner和Grete Lechner按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Josef Lechner喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001352:child; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1941.05** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.listen-coins` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -1.2 (让出的加班费); reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Josef Lechner嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Josef Lechner用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; reputation 0.04_

- **1941.11** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Anna Weiss来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef Lechner切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1942.01** `p9-history-cultural-contact.join-circle/circle-rejects` tags: culture, knowledge, letter, lecture, editor, training, circle, task
  有人上下打量Josef Lechner的衣领，低声说了句什么，桌边笑起来。Josef Lechner把杯子放下，咖啡还剩一半。Franz Weiss送他到门口，说“别在意”，没有提下星期四。
  _effects: cash -1 (咖啡钱); mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  Josef Lechner把新东家的信放在柜台上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne读完，抽出公司的信笺，蘸墨水写下“勤勉可靠，品行端正，因故离柜，并无纠葛”。写完吹干，盖了章，还多写一句“有需要可来函”。Josef Lechner把信纸夹进厚纸板，第二天一早就往玛丽亚希尔夫的新账房去了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +has_strong_reference_

  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Grete Stein 2用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef Lechner才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +parish_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  Josef Lechner上楼敲了Franz Hofer的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Franz Hofer把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Josef Lechner能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001337:child; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1943.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef Lechner没有立刻答话，先去敲Anna Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anna Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef Lechner把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  Josef Lechner在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef Lechner去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

  Josef Lechner咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Josef Lechner手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Berta Lechner 2等在门口，问值不值，Josef Lechner只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.01999999999999999; skill_

- **1944.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Anton攥着Josef Lechner的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1944.05** `statistical_infection`
  Josef Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1944.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef Lechner从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  Josef Lechner只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008104_

- **1948.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Josef Lechner把报纸推到一边，和Helene Bauer聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Josef Lechner起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000707:child; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.01** `r14-edu.music-teacher-legacy::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/inherited-the-scores` tags: education, arts_training, music, legacy, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Anton Stein退休前把一箱琴谱交给Josef Lechner，说里面有些是自己抄的，错音不少，别照着弹。Josef Lechner翻到一本旧练习曲，页边有铅笔写的指法，还有几处被橡皮擦破的洞。Anton Stein说这些谱子跟了自己四十年，现在该换个地方落灰了。箱底还有三克朗纸币。
  _effects: cash 3 (琴谱边上的铅笔字); flag +inherited_music_scores; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005210_

- **1949.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Josef Lechner一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Helene Weiss 3送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  Josef Lechner把画挂在画廊最里面的墙角，标签上只写了“无题”。三天里，来看的人从画前走过去，停在旁边的风景画前。闭展时，Josef Lechner把画取下来，画框背面落了一层灰。
  _effects: flag +painting_ignored_at_exhibition_

- **1949.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef Lechner去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Otto Berg路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1949.11** `r15-org.workshop-closure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/closed` tags: work, factory, organization, unemployment, collective, vienna, urban, postwar
  老板在早班前把所有人叫到院子里，说厂子撑不下去了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne没有看任何人的眼睛。中午之前，封条贴上了车间门。Josef Lechner把工具柜里的东西装进纸袋，发现里面还有两年前的一双工作手套。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; unemployment (车间门口的封条); state_add purge-ongoing state:world-vienna-1912:81; state_add blacklisted state:world-vienna-1912:82; flag +blacklisted_

- **1949.12** `r3-mig.citizenship-oath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university::reform-new-state-oath/oath-postponed-letter` tags: migration, citizenship, oath, papers, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Berg把入籍证书和出生证明并排放在桌上，说中间少了一个字母，不能宣誓。Josef Lechner说那个字母是德文拼法，Katharina Berg说“这里只认拉丁字母的拼法”。仪式改到下个月，Anna Moser 2白请了半天假，Mitzi Kral把准备好的花又带回去了。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; condition; flag +oath_postponed_name_discrepancy; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; knowledge “a single letter can postpone naturalization”_

- **1950.03** `r15-work.factory-hiring::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/hired` tags: work, factory, employment, industrial, wage, vienna, urban, postwar
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Josef Lechner的名字写在班次表上。工钱不高，工时很长，但下个星期开始有固定的收入。Josef Lechner回家时绕路买了一小块肥皂。
  _effects: employment factory worker @ org-winter-print; state_add factory-employment state:world-vienna-1912:89; flag -blacklisted_

- **1950.05** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef Lechner对Gustav Winter 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Gustav Winter 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Josef Lechner的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1950.05** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Gustav Lechner 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Josef Lechner留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Josef Lechner在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1950.05** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/not-king-material` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Rosa Stein 2看了Josef Lechner的手，说这双手适合拿账本，不适合拿权杖。Josef Lechner回家路上买了一张最便宜的站票，在最后一排看完了整场戏；散场时，Josef Lechner记住的不是国王的台词，而是他坐下时椅子发出的声音。
  _effects: skill; cash -1 (买了一张站票（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +cast_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1951.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Josef Lechner不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Josef Lechner拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Josef Lechner的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); cash -6 (medical treatment（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/hurt` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick, injury
  Josef Lechner在换班时被机器擦伤了手臂。工头让她去水龙头下面冲一冲，然后回来继续。
  _effects: health -0.05 (factory accident); condition; state_parameter factory-employment.injured false -> true_

- **1951.01** `r13-fam.apologize::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/apology-rejected` tags: task, social, apology, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Lechner把带来的半块肥皂放在窗台上，说知道不够。Josef没有碰肥皂，只说那天在场的人都记得Josef Lechner笑的样子。Josef Lechner站到腿发麻，最后把肥皂收回口袋，转身时听见窗子从里面关上了。
  _effects: flag +apology_rejected; relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1951.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Maria Huber把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef Lechner去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  两家人为嫁妆争到天黑。Anna Stein说家里只拿得出一只箱子，Rosa Berg 2的母亲把茶碗放下，说那就再等等。Rosa Berg 2把Josef Lechner拉到厨房，说没有嫁妆也嫁。Josef Lechner看见Rosa Berg 2的手在抖。
  _effects: cash 3 (订婚宴上的两副碗筷); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; role; role_

  Josef Lechner在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef Lechner把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Josef Lechner拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Anna Stein 2把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Josef Lechner把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; organization_funds_

  房东站在楼梯转角数钱，数完把钥匙串往手心里磕了磕，说涨价这一成是行情。看门人拎着工具箱上来，锁芯换了回去，门一关，院子里的床架才搬回屋里。Josef Lechner把剩下的克朗一张张压在碟子底下。
  _effects: cash -20 (补齐部分欠租才换回门锁（其中20克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +rent_arrears_partial; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

  Katharina Hofer 2说丈夫是“在睡梦中走的”，但邻居告诉Josef Lechner，死者是在酒馆后巷冻死的。Josef Lechner把两种说法都写进稿子，Gustav Winter 2删掉了后一句。报纸出来时，Katharina Hofer 2在报社门口站了很久，手里攥着那份讣告。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +printed_unverified_obituary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; knowledge “the family version and the police report disagree on the cau”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000146:child; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1954.01** `kernel-family.inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/inheritance.equal-split` tags: task, task:family, family, inheritance, siblings, estate, law, vienna
  办事处二楼的暖气不热，Katharina Berg把父亲的存款清单摊在桌上：八十四克朗，扣除丧葬费还剩六十二。Josef Lechner和Anna Lechner一人一半；Anna Lechner先把先前从父亲铁盒里拿走的十五克朗放回桌上，余下的十六克朗由办事处补。出门后两人往相反方向走，约好清明一起上坟。

  Berta Huber 3在“身份”一栏写了“房客”，说这个类别每三个月要重新登记。Josef Lechner问为什么不能写“住户”，Berta Huber 3说住户要有租约和门牌。Anna Moser 2在旁边小声提醒，三个月后来盖章要带一克朗。

  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004691:child_

  _effects: cash -2 (诊所缝合的费用); reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162_

  _effects: cash -8 (半砖界墙（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +won_wall_dispute; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; knowledge “the court ordered the boundary wall moved back half a brick”_

  舅父听完摇了摇头，说钱不借，倒可以给他写张条子，介绍一个跑布吕恩的车夫，让他搭便车走。舅母把点心收进橱里，说路上总得带点吃的。他收了条子，坐车夫的车走了两天。

- **1964.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef Lechner把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Josef Lechner自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1964.07** `statistical_infection`
  Josef Lechner在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1964.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef Lechner的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1964.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef Lechner从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  车票钱只够坐到布吕恩。他在车站帮人搬箱子、给旅馆跑腿，换了几顿饭和半张到家乡的票，晚上睡在候车室的长椅上。到家时靴底磨穿了一个洞，他拄着一根捡来的木棍走进院子。
  _effects: cash -7 (到布吕恩的车票与半张后续车票（其中7克朗记入债务）); skill; condition_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-196; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-196_

  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  _effects: cash -25 (private consultation（其中22.2克朗记入债务）); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; role_

  Josef Lechner和Maria Bauer 2在第一个酒棚就坐下了，要了一壶新酒，Friedrich Huber摸出一副磨损的纸牌。三人打到下午，太阳偏西也没再往上爬；Josef Lechner输了几个铜板，连酒带牌局一共一克朗五十赫勒，倒赢回一肚子笑话。回程的电车上，Josef Lechner靠着窗，酒意上来，差点坐过站。

  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (补鞋底的开销（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。
